# INVENTORY — Fix program #16: all model pins Grok, scrub Claude/Codex/Anthropic/Opus

- **Date:** 2026-11-11 (inventory only; no plugin file was edited, `build.sh` was not run, the Campus Eats artifact was not touched)
- **Scope inventoried:** staged sources (`new-skills/`, `round2-skills/`, `~/workspace/cursor-skills/less-flat-ui/`), `build.sh`, `LIVE_SMOKE_TEST.md`, `README.md` (repo root), `.cursor-plugin/marketplace.json`, and the built tree (`hackathon-ecc/` — `agents/`, `rules/`, `commands/`, `skills/`, `scripts/`, `hooks/`, plus `schemas/`, `manifests/`, `README.md`, `mcp.json`).
- **Search terms (case-insensitive):** `composer-2.5`, `claude`, `codex`, `anthropic`, `opus`, `gemini`, `gpt-`, `sonnet`, `haiku`. Raw grep output: `/tmp/fix16/all-hits.txt` (3,672 lines). Classifier: `/tmp/fix16/classify.py`; classified rows: `/tmp/fix16/classified.tsv`.
- **Baseline:** `build.sh` is **23,217 lines, md5 `1f1a232572b9e12332733688838e1233`** — MATCHES the fix #15 state. (See §8.)

## 1. Verified model table — use exactly these, do not invent slugs

| Display name | Pin slug (exact) | Source | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Grok 4.7 High | `grok-4.7-high` | Cursor Docs `cursor.com/help/models-and-usage/grok-4-7`; fix #13 established the pin; corroborated by a Cursor forum usage-dashboard post showing bare `grok-4.7-high` as a real model ID in usage exports | First-party Cursor model; four effort levels low/medium/high/xhigh, named-model default high; API id `grok-4.7`. **Slug-only, never bracket syntax** for Grok. Per-vendor key is `reasoning_effort`, NOT `effort`. Dropdown-coupling caveat stands: Cursor's Task tool only offers ONE effort level per model, whichever is currently selected in the picker — a pin can fail or silently fall back unless the named model is selected in the picker; the live smoke test decides. |
| Grok 4.6 High Fast | `cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast` | Cursor Docs `cursor.com/help/models-and-usage/grok-4-6` ("Fast is the default speed tier on Premium and higher"; price table lists "Grok 4.6 (Fast)" $4/$12); negraru CursorBench table; PromptConduit rate card; attested as `cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast` in the Cursor forum cloud-subagent allowlist (`forum.cursor.com/t/limited-number-of-models-for-cloud-subagents/168759/13`), Cursor usage exports, and Cursor CLI live tests (`agent --list-models` gives `cursor-grok-4.6-{low,medium,high,xhigh}[-fast]`) | **The bare form `grok-4.6-high-fast` is NOT attested in any source — DO NOT use it anywhere.** The `cursor-` prefix is part of the pin. Four effort levels, named-model default high, Fast is the default speed tier on Premium and higher. |
| (pre-existing, unchanged) Grok 4.7 High design-lead pin | `grok-4.7-high` | Fix #13 (see §7) | Already in place in orchestrate row 10 / step 4, `orch-build-mvp` step 4, `orch-change-feature`, `gan-build.md`, `gan-design.md`, both READMEs, `LIVE_SMOKE_TEST.md` §14. **Zero `grok-4.7-high-fast` or `grok-4.7[` hits anywhere** — pin form still holds (verified: `grep -rn 'grok-4.7\[' hackathon-ecc/ new-skills/` returns 0). |

**Current Grok footprint (pre-fix):** `grok-4.7-high` appears 53 times total — built tree 15, `new-skills/` 4, `build.sh` 34, `round2-skills/` 0. **`cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast` and `grok-4.6` appear ZERO times anywhere** — the fast-tier slug is entirely new to the tree.

## 2. Role → pin mapping

Rule applied: current **composer tier → `cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast`** (smaller task bot); current **opus tier → `grok-4.7-high`** (orchestrator / design / implementation / reasoning); **design-lead already `grok-4.7-high`** (no change). The orchestrator itself is the session main model (user-chosen); Anish's instruction makes the main orchestrator and design+implementation bot Grok 4.7 High, so the picker instruction for the main session model becomes "select Grok 4.7 High" wherever the skill text says the lead model is user-chosen (orchestrate SKILL.md:14 — see §6-F2).

## 4. "Composer" disambiguation — what stays vs what goes

The word "Composer" in the tree is THREE different things; only one goes:

| Meaning | Occurrences | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| `composer-2.5` / `composer-2.5[fast=false]` — the Cursor model pin | 240 hit lines | **GOES** → `cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast` (§2) |
| PHP Composer (package manager) — `composer audit/validate/--version/dump-autoload`, `composer.json`, `composer.lock`, `getComposerDeps`, capital-C "Composer package name / Composer checks" in `laravel-plugin-discovery`, `laravel-verification`, `php-security.mdc`, `scripts/lib/project-detect.js`, `scripts/lib/gateguard-search-filters.js` | 6 capital-C + ~15 lowercase CLI uses (not counted in the 3,672 — they matched no search term except via `composer` in the disambiguation grep) | **STAYS** — unrelated to the model; it is the PHP tool |
| English word "composer" — "it is not a free-form team composer" (`dev-team/SKILL.md:14`), "Reply composer cues" (`ui-ux-pro-max/data/ui-reasoning.csv:165`), CSS class `.composer` (`scripts/lib/plan-canvas/ui.js:171-172`), `remotion-composer/` (`pitch-deck-video` references) | ~6 | **STAYS** — ordinary words / identifiers, not the model |
| Cursor's Composer UI feature (Agent/Composer mode) | **0 mentions found anywhere** | Nothing to distinguish; no occurrence exists |

Similarly: `ngrok` in `hackathon-scope/SKILL.md:75` ("Demo from laptop, ngrok if needed") is NOT Grok — stays. The `x.ai ('Meet Grok Bot')` demo copy in `design-taste-frontend` assets (`heroes.json:823`, `moment.js`, `cards.md:4`) refers to xAI's Grok product as demo content, not a model pin — stays (flag if the coordinator wants demo copy scrubbed too).

## 5. Functional-dependency proposals

General rule used: **replace with the Grok/Cursor equivalent where one exists and is named exactly; otherwise remove cleanly and name what is lost.** Where no equivalent exists and removal would break a feature, that is stated, not papered over.

### F1. Multi-host installer — the biggest scope decision [FLAG §6-F10]

- **Where:** `scripts/lib/install/*` (incl. `install-targets/claude-home.js`, `codex-home.js`, `gemini-project.js`, `copilot-project.js`, `opencode-home.js`, `qwen-home.js`, `adal/antigravity/codebuddy/hermes/joycode/kimi/openclaw/zed`), `scripts/lib/claude-plugin-setup.js` (65 hits — top file), `scripts/lib/codex-plugin-setup.js` (38), `scripts/lib/claude-scope-migration.js` (26), `scripts/lib/codex-legacy-sync.js` (46), `scripts/lib/install/apply.js` (43), `install-lifecycle.js` (31), `claude-settings*.js`, `codex-user-config.js`, `claude-skill-migration.js`, `claude-settings-lock.js`, `opencode-install-lock.js`, `manifests/install-modules.json` (48 hits), `manifests/install-profiles.json`, `manifests/pi-core.json` (51), `schemas/*` target enums, `scripts/lib/context-profiles*.js`, `scripts/ci/validate-skill-host-compat.js` (37).
- **What it is:** the plugin installs itself into **17 harnesses** (`context-carrier.schema.json:13` target enum: adal, antigravity, claude, claude-project, codebuddy, codex, copilot, cursor, gemini, hermes, joycode, kimi, openclaw, opencode, pi, qwen, zed).
- **Proposal:** strip to Cursor-only — delete the non-Cursor install-target files, remove non-Cursor entries from the target enums/manifests/schemas, delete `claude-plugin-setup.js` / `codex-plugin-setup.js` / `codex-legacy-sync.js` / `claude-scope-migration.js` and their callers. Keep `install-targets/cursor-project.js`, `registry.js`, `helpers.js`.
- **What is lost:** installing/running the plugin under Claude Code, Codex, Gemini CLI, Copilot, OpenCode, Qwen, Pi, and the other 10 harnesses. This is a product-scope decision, not a rename — the upstream ECC plugin is multi-host by design.
- **Alternative (not recommended if the scrub is literal):** keep the installer but reword — this would leave live Claude/Codex code paths in the tree and violate "scrub ALL mentions".

### F2. Session adapters

- **Where:** `scripts/lib/session-adapters/claude-history.js`, `codex-worktree.js`, `opencode.js`, `dmux-tmux.js`, `canonical-session.js`, `registry.js`; `scripts/lib/session-manager.js`, `session-aliases.js`, `session-bridge.js`, `orchestration-session.js`, `observer-sessions.js`.
- **Proposal:** remove the `claude-history`, `codex-worktree`, `opencode` adapters; keep `canonical-session`, `dmux-tmux` (tmux is host-neutral), Cursor-side session handling, and the registry entries for what remains.
- **What is lost:** resuming/reading sessions that live in Claude Code (`~/.claude` history) or Codex worktrees, and OpenCode sessions.

### F3. Context profiles / native CLI discovery

- **Where:** `scripts/lib/context-profile-native.js` (53 hits — includes a backend table with `codex: {command: 'codex', requiredVersion: '0.154.0'…}` and `claude: {command: 'claude', requiredVersion: '2.1.292'…}`), `context-profile-native-executable.js`, `context-profile-native-discovery.js`, `context-profile-*.js`, `context-profiles.js`, `context-carriers.js`, `context-pack-registry.js`, `context-selection.js`.
- **Proposal:** remove the `codex`/`claude`/`gemini`/other non-Cursor backend entries from the native discovery table; keep Cursor entries only.
- **What is lost:** context carriers / profiles for non-Cursor harnesses.

- **Proposal:** delete the non-Cursor readers; keep the Cursor reader (verify which reader that is — `collect.js` dispatches by host).
- **What is lost:** inventorying MCP servers configured in Claude Code / Codex / OpenCode.

### F13. Hook labels and hook mechanics

- The 36 files under `scripts/`+`hooks/` (37 occurrences) and 196 further occurrences elsewhere carry the "Claude Code-only" label. Rewording the label to "Cursor-only" is only honest where the hook genuinely runs in Cursor. Several labelled items are Claude-Code-only mechanics with no Cursor equivalent (e.g. `PreToolUse`/`PostToolUse` hook events in `continuous-learning-v2/SKILL.md:41,86`) — those need the feature decision, not a relabel [FLAG — per-item, see Appendix B (d) rows].
- `hooks/adapter.js:8` claims the adapter "is live infrastructure — not a Claude Code-only or unwired script" — that sentence is itself a (d)-adjacent adjudicated label; the file's `transformToClaude` function (§6-F9) is the real question.

### F14. Install/state/config code that references Claude paths

- **Where:** `scripts/lib/utils.js` / `agent-data-home.js` (`getClaudeDir`, `~/.claude` fallbacks), `scripts/lib/resolve-ecc-root.js` (30 hits — resolution order `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` → `~/.claude/` → `~/.claude/plugins/…`; step 6 already notes "finds the plugin in a Cursor install, where ~/.claude does not exist"), `scripts/lib/install/*` (consent, guarded-write, inventory, plan, runtime), `scripts/setup-package-manager.js`, `scripts/lib/package-manager.js`.
- **Proposal:** re-point root resolution at the Cursor plugin location first; drop `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT` (keep `ECC_PLUGIN_ROOT`); do NOT invent Cursor home paths — where Cursor's equivalent of `~/.claude` is unverified, flag it rather than guessing (`~/.cursor` is used in Appendix B proposals only as a flagged placeholder, marked VERIFY).

### F15. Everything else (c) — mechanical rewording

- 1,696 descriptive lines: "Claude Code" → "Cursor", "Claude" → "Cursor" (where it means the host), "Anthropic" → delete/reword (attribution sentences), "Codex/Gemini" → delete the sentence or the feature reference, "Opus/Sonnet/Haiku" as model words → Grok tier names. Upstream attributions that name the original project (`claude-code-orchestrate`, Taste skill by Leonxlnx, Anthropic's March 2026 harness paper, `everything-claude-code` legacy slug `LEGACY_PLUGIN_SLUG`) need a coordinator call on whether attribution keeps the upstream name [FLAG §6-F11].

## 7. Fix #13 slug-evidence verification

Read `fix13/MANIFEST.md`, `fix13/MANIFEST-A.md`, `fix13/MANIFEST-B.md` and the fix13 scripts:

- `MANIFEST.md:85` — "Pin `grok-4.7-high` (slug form only, NO bracket syntax anywhere — verified: zero `grok-4.7[` hits in the built tree)".
- `MANIFEST.md:103` — "`grok-4.7-high` in orchestrate SKILL.md: 4 hits (:28, :57, :86, :154); `grok-4.7[` in built skills: 0."
- `MANIFEST-A.md:50` — "Pin `grok-4.7-high` (slug form only, NO bracket syntax anywhere — verified: `grep -rn \"grok-4.7\\[\" new-skills/ rewritten-skills/` returns nothing)".
- `MANIFEST.md:25` — pre-fix #13 state: "No `grok-4.7-high` pin existed anywhere (grep \"grok\" returned only Grok-logo icon files). Design slices dispatched through orchestrate tiers pinned at :25-27 (deep-reasoner `claude-opus-5`, fast-worker `composer-2.5[fast=false]`, scout `composer-2.5`)".
- **Re-verified now:** `grep -rn 'grok-4.7\[' hackathon-ecc/ new-skills/` returns **0** — the pin form still holds. `grok-4.7-high` count is 53 across the inventoried set (built tree 15, `new-skills/` 4, `build.sh` 34). The `reasoning_effort` note (per-vendor key is `reasoning_effort`, NOT `effort`; Cursor does not expose it in a pin, so the pin is the slug alone) is recorded at `new-skills/orchestrate/SKILL.md:158` and must be carried onto the new fast-tier pins too (no bracket form for `cursor-grok-4.6-high-fast` either — the `cursor-` prefix is part of the slug, not a bracket).

## 8. Baseline build.sh check

- `build.sh`: **23,217 lines, md5 `1f1a232572b9e12332733688838e1233`** — **MATCHES** the fix #15 state. Verified by `wc -l` + `md5sum` at inventory start.
- `build.sh` hit profile: 958 hit lines — 174 (a), 376 (b), 295 (c), 113 (d). Distribution: 917 hits in lines < 20,000 (embedded plugin content), 41 hits in lines > 21,500 (fix scripts, e.g. the fix9 sweep-registry print at :21903/:21922 naming `codex@0.167.1`).
- **CRITICAL — build.sh is NOT a 1:1 mirror of the built tree:**
  - `model: composer-2.5[fast=false]` appears **122× in build.sh vs 63× in the live sources** (61 built agents + staged `fast-worker.md` + built `fast-worker.md`) — content is embedded roughly twice (heredoc writes + Python fix scripts that re-embed file contents as string literals).
  - `model: claude-opus-5[effort=high]` appears **9× in build.sh and 0× as a live pin in the built tree** (the built tree's 2 `effort=high` hits are the `deep-reasoner.md:7` comment describing the form as REMOVED). A bare `model: opus` appears **1× in build.sh (:7788, in an astra-review variant delegating to a "Fable" subagent) and 0× in the built tree.** `Model: gpt-6-astra` appears **2× in build.sh (:7836, :7849 — two astra-review variants)** vs 1× in the built tree.
  - `grok-4.7-high` appears **34× in build.sh** vs 19× in the live sources.
  - **Consequence:** patching only the built tree is not enough — every embedding in `build.sh` must be patched too, or the next rebuild reintroduces the old pins (including the stale `[effort=high]` and bare-`opus` variants that exist nowhere else).
